Welcome to the *Dungeons & Dragons: Adventures in the Forgotten Realms* Class Challenge! From July 14 through July 21, this new type of *Magic Online* event offers you a choice from twelve different preconstructed decks built around each Class enchantment and compete against other players in a League!

![](https://media.wizards.com/2021/images/daily/tgxLN6WTIw.jpg)

Read on for event details, philosophy, and decklists.

# Event Details

## *Adventures in the Forgotten Realms* Class Challenge

**Dates**: July 14–21

**Format**: A league of up to three Best-of-One, single game rounds.

**Phantom**: Yes

**Location**: In the Limited Play Lobby, Gauntlet Section

**Entry**: 60 Play Points or 6 Event Tickets

**Prizes:**

<table><tbody><tr><td>&#10;			<p>3 wins</p>&#10;			</td>&#10;			<td>&#10;			<p>90 Play Points and 1 Treasure Chest</p>&#10;			</td>&#10;		</tr><tr><td>&#10;			<p>2 wins</p>&#10;			</td>&#10;			<td>&#10;			<p>60 Play Points</p>&#10;			</td>&#10;		</tr><tr><td>&#10;			<p>1 win</p>&#10;			</td>&#10;			<td>&#10;			<p>30 Play Points</p>&#10;			</td>&#10;		</tr></tbody></table>

# Choose Your Character

Similar to previous Gauntlet events on *Magic Online*, you played your matches against other players with randomly distributed decks. However now, thanks to the awesome team behind *Magic Online*, you can play with a deck of your choosing! This lets us feature all kinds of formats in the future using *Magic Online*’s large card pool, from Standard decks throughout the years, to Commander, and even to new formats such as this one.

We wanted to find a way to highlight the new cards in *Adventures in the Forgotten Realms*, while also leveraging *Magic Online*’s strengths—namely its massive card pool. Creating twelve unique decklists, each with their own playstyles and featuring the twelve Class enchantments from the set, highlights this newfound *Magic Online* agency by mirroring one of the very first choices you make when playing *Dungeons & Dragons*: choosing your class!

We’re always looking for ways to bring you different kinds of sweet experiences, and we hope this new way to play *Magic* brings you joy. You can always let us know your thoughts by emailing magiconlinefeedback@wizards.com or mentioning [@MagicOnline](https://twitter.com/MagicOnline) on Twitter.

Thank you for sticking with us, and may you find the class that resonates the most with you!

# Class Challenge Decklists

## Barbarian

Roll the dice in this Blue-Red deck as a Path of Wild Magic Barbarian, and try your luck leaping into combat, kicking in doors, and feeling the rage!

## Bard

As a Bard of the College of Valor, sing the song of red and green legendary creatures throughout *Magic*'s history coming together to defeat their foes with their combined might!

## Cleric

In this White-Black Control deck, death is only a minor inconvenience for you, a Grave Domain Cleric, as you'll gain life almost as fast as your opponents lose theirs!



## Druid

As a Circle of the Land Druid, you will be using the power of lands to do anything from drawing cards to attacking in this land-based Green-Blue deck.

## Fighter

A Fighter is only as good as their weapon, and in this deck you have mastered them all! Find the right equipment for the job and show your foes the power of steel.

## Monk

As a Monk studying the Way of the Open Hand, you'll be casting multiple spells each turn in this White-Blue Tempo deck. Your opponents will be feeling your Flurry of Blows as you amass a token army using your Ki Empowered spells!

#### Monk

## Paladin

As a Paladin who has sworn the Oath of Devotion, you will call upon your Planeswalker allies to amass a token army that will uphold the ideals of justice, virtue, and order in this Green-White deck.

#### Paladin

#####

## Ranger

As a Beast Master Hunter, you will fight with the allies of nature. Cast creature after creature to protect civilization and those you hold dear in this Green Aggressive deck. Your Favored Enemy? Opponents!

#### Ranger

## Rogue

While most Rogues work alone, you have more allies than they'll ever expect. In this Blue-Black Rogue Tribal deck, you'll use every Cunning Action available to steal your opponent's hope, coin, and life.

#### Rogue

## Sorcerer

Magic is power. As a Storm Sorcerer, you'll be harnessing the power of Instants and Sorceries in this Blue-Red deck. Use them to create tokens big and small, and rain destruction upon all those who would underestimate your power.

#### Sorcerer

## Warlock

You've traded a drop of humanity for a sea of power, and you'll never look back. In this Mono-Black deck, you'll be using your eldritch gifts to blast foes, summon powerful allies, and show the world what it means to be a Warlock.

## Wizard

As a Wizard studying the School of Conjuration, you've trained your whole life to draw cards and benefit from this knowledge. You'll protect the coast where you live using creatures enhanced by your knowledge and magical prowess in this Mono-Blue control deck.

#### Wizard
